  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子霍尔流体中的Wen-Zee型混合规范-曲率耦合推广至广义相对论语境，特别是黑洞视界热力学，以探索拓扑响应与引力熵的深层联系。

Method: 基于Maxwell代数的三维Chern-Simons引力构造洛伦兹协变Wen-Zee型耦合；运用Wald熵公式计算该边界项对黑洞视界熵的贡献；将结果诠释为视界上的horizon boost charge，通过环路积分与Abel holonomy分析其几何本质；在平直宇宙学解与AdS-Lorentz黑洞精确解上进行显式计算。

Result: Wen-Zee型耦合在Chern-Simons引力中诱导非平凡的Wald熵贡献，即视界boost电荷，表达为额外U(1)规范场沿分叉圆切向的环路积分；该电荷即使在混合项运动方程满足（on-shell）时仍可非零；当投影方向协变常数时，该积分等价于Abel规范场的holonomy；在AdS-Lorentz黑洞上得到有限、温度依赖的熵修正。

Conclusion: Wen-Zee响应机制可系统推广至引力理论，其对应的视界boost电荷构成一类新型几何熵贡献，架起了凝聚态拓扑序与黑洞热力学之间的概念桥梁。

Abstract: The Wen-Zee term is a mixed gauge-curvature coupling in the topological Chern-Simons description of a quantum Hall fluid, encoding the electric charge bound to spatial curvature. We investigate a Lorentz-covariant generalisation of this coupling, expanding its scope to black-hole spacetimes and determine its contribution to horizon entropy. Such a coupling arises naturally in three-dimensional Chern-Simons gravity based on the Maxwell algebra. We show that its Wald entropy contribution is a horizon boost charge, given by a loop integral of the additional gauge field projected along the tangent to the bifurcation circle. This contribution can be nonzero even when the mixed term vanishes on shell. We show that the integral admits an Abelian holonomy interpretation when the projection direction is covariantly constant. We evaluate the charge on a flat cosmological solution and, using smooth horizon fields and a specified thermal ensemble, on an AdS-Lorentz black hole. These results connect the geometric response familiar from quantum Hall physics to gravitational horizon thermodynamics.

</details>


### [102] [Majorana bilinears, compact modular seeds and the approach to the Tsirelson bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04729)
*M. S. Guimaraes,I. Roditi,S. P. Sorella*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了1+1维自由大质量Majorana场真空态下Bell-CHSH不等式的违背，利用Rindler左右楔形区域中由模频率种子函数通过Tomita-Takesaki算子构造的偶次双线性可观测量，得到Bell参数闭式解；当种子为单紧支 bumps 的相位旋转时，可观测量生成Pauli代数副本，精确计算了真空限制下的关联矩阵；最优设置突破经典界限的谱宽超平面设置两倍以上；证明楔形真空无法达到Tsirelson界，趋近该界要求Bell矢量的模谱权集中在模算子本征值λ=1处，从而精确定量实现了Summers-Werner机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究相对论性量子场论中真空纠缠结构如何体现非定域性，特别是自由费米子场在Rindler分解下能否实现强Bell违背，并检验Summers-Werner机制在具体模型中的实现方式。

Method: 采用代数QFT框架，以Rindler左右楔形区域的偶次双线性局域可观测量作为Bell测试的二元观测算符；利用Tomita-Takesaki理论构造模频率表象下的测试函数：右侧楔形用Δ^{i s}作用种子，左侧楔形用i Δ^{-i s}作用（体现扭曲对偶性）；对正模频率支撑的种子，计算单粒子重叠与真空关联矩阵；将种子取为紧支bump的相位旋转，导出Pauli代数表示并解析求解CHSH相关量。

Result: 获得Bell参数S的闭式表达；证明当测试函数由相位旋转紧支种子生成时，S可超越经典界限达2√2以下，且最优谱宽>2×平面波设置；严格证明S<2√2恒成立，且lim S→2√2 ⇔ Bell矢量的模谱测度弱收敛至δ(λ−1)；确认自由Majorana场偶扇区完全实现Summers-Werner机制。

Conclusion: 自由Majorana场真空在Rindler分解下能产生强但非最大化的Bell违背，其强度由模算子谱结构决定；Tsirelson界不可达性源于楔形代数的类型III_1性质及模算子连续谱，而λ=1点的谱集中是逼近最大违背的必要条件；该结果为QFT中真空非定域性的代数起源提供了严格范例。

Abstract: In this work we study the Bell-CHSH inequality in the vacuum of the free massive Majorana field in $1+1$ dimensions, taking as the dichotomic observables of the two parties even bilinears of smeared fields localized in the right and in the left Rindler wedges. The test functions are generated from seeds in the modular-frequency variable through the Tomita-Takesaki operator of the right wedge and, on the side of the left wedge, through its adjoint multiplied by $i$, which accounts for twisted duality. For seeds supported at positive modular frequency the one-particle overlaps between the two parties do not depend on the modular weights, and we obtain the Bell parameter in closed form. When the seeds are phase rotations of a single compact bump, the bilinears generate a copy of the Pauli algebra in each wedge, and we compute exactly the correlation matrix of the vacuum restricted to the two copies. The optimal settings violate the classical bound up to spectral widths more than twice as large as those reached with planar settings. We also show that the Tsirelson bound is never attained in the wedge vacuum for any choice of dichotomic observables, and that approaching it forces the modular spectral weight of the Bell vectors to concentrate at the spectral point $λ=1$ of the modular operator. This establishes in a precise way how the even sector of the free Majorana field realizes the Summers-Werner mechanism.

</details>


### [103] [An Observer in de Sitter Space, and Rereading Everett](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04804)
*Edward Witten*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Hugh Everett的经典文章，从局域观测者的视角探讨德西特空间的熵，强调在宇宙学中观测者本身是被观测宇宙的一部分这一关键事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强调在宇宙学问题中，观测者并非外部独立实体，而是宇宙系统内部的一部分，因此必须将观测者纳入量子引力与热力学分析框架中。

Method: 基于Everett多世界诠释的量子力学框架，结合德西特时空的因果结构与局域观测者视界（cosmological horizon），采用量子态约化与信息熵定义分析熵的本体论含义。

Result: 指出德西特熵本质上是局域观测者对其不可达区域所缺失信息的量化，而非全局几何量；该熵具有内在的主观察依赖性与有限性。

Conclusion: 德西特熵是一个主观察依赖的、非全局的热力学量，其定义必须严格基于特定观测者的因果视界与量子测量过程，从而支持'宇宙即量子系统，观测者即子系统'的基本图景。

Abstract: After re-examining a classic article by Hugh Everett, I will discuss the entropy of de Sitter space from the standpoint of an observer living in the space. This provides a nice example of the importance -- for certain questions -- of taking seriously the fact that in cosmology any observer is really part of the universe that is being observed. (This article has appeared in a special issue of International Journal of Modern Physics A in honor of Gerard 't Hooft.

</details>


### [104] [Scalar potential bounds on one-parameter Calabi-Yau threefolds with fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04858)
*Nana Geraldine Cabo Bizet,José de J. Pérez Lona*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了IIB型弦理论在一参数Calabi-Yau三维流形紧化下的多场通量势中，精炼de Sitter猜想（RdSC）、跨普朗克 censorship 猜想（TCC）及物种尺度Λ_s的行为；通过精确求解Picard-Fuchs方程，在镜像五次曲面模型中扫描200组通量构型，发现96%存在不稳定临界点；RdSC第一条件因no-scale结构在所有临界点自动满足（|∇V|/V = √6）；TCC导出势梯度在大复结构极限为2、锥形奇点发散、axio-dilaton方向恒为√2，并给出紧化体积下界v > v₀ ∼ e^N；最终TCC体积约束比物种尺度场范围约束更严格，且蕴含V ≲ 3Λ_s²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验弦论背景下de Sitter真空的可能性与稳定性，特别是RdSC和TCC在精确多场通量势中的实现程度，并厘清其与物种尺度Λ_s及紧化几何的定量关系。

Method: 基于一参数Calabi-Yau三重曲面（如镜像五次曲面）的精确周期（由Picard-Fuchs方程解出），构造含axio-dilaton与单复结构模的no-scale多场通量势；数值扫描通量构型以统计临界点性质；解析推导TCC要求的势梯度|∇V|/V在不同区域（大复结构、锥形奇点、SL(2)-协变axio-dilaton子空间）的渐进行为；结合Kähler runaway与体积依赖的Λ_s，分析TCC与物种尺度约束的相对强度。

Result: 1) RdSC第一条件|∇V|/V ≥ c' 在所有临界点严格满足，c'=0.18（统计平均），且因no-scale结构实际取普适值√6 ≈ 2.45；2) TCC要求的|∇V|/V在axio-dilaton方向恒为√2，在大复结构极限趋近2，在锥形奇点发散；3) TCC推出紧化体积下界v > v₀ ∼ e^N；4) 场位移满足物种尺度场范围约束，但TCC体积约束更强，并隐含标量势上界V ≲ 3Λ_s²。

Conclusion: 在该类IIB通量紧化中，RdSC第一条件天然满足，TCC对紧化体积施加强约束，且其效应压倒物种尺度场范围限制；锥形奇点附近梯度发散支持TCC，而axio-dilaton方向的SL(2)协变结构是TCC成立的关键几何特征；结果表明de Sitter真空若存在，必位于强耦合或小体积区域，且受TCC严格限制。

Abstract: We study the refined de Sitter conjecture (RdSC), the Trans-Planckian Censorship Conjecture (TCC) and the species scale $Λ_s$ in the multifield flux potential of type IIB string theory compactified on one-parameter Calabi--Yau threefolds. The scalar potential depends on the axio-dilaton and on the single complex-structure modulus, and is no-scale along the Kähler directions. Restricting to $h^{2,1}=1$ allows us to work with the exact periods obtained from the Picard--Fuchs equations rather than with their large-complex-structure expansion, and therefore to probe the conifold region. For the mirror quintic, $(h^{2,1},h^{1,1})=(1,101)$, we scan 200 flux configurations and find unstable extrema in $96\%$ of the cases, with $c'=0.18$. Once the Kähler runaway is taken into account, the no-scale structure fixes the logarithmic slope of the potential at every critical point to the universal value $\sqrt6$, so that the first RdSC condition is satisfied throughout. Statistically, the complex structure is the heavier sector and the axio-dilaton dominates the unstable directions. For the TCC we obtain analytic results valid for the whole class of one-parameter models: the slope approaches the universal value $2$ at large complex structure, diverges at the conifold, and equals exactly $\sqrt2$ in the axio-dilaton directions, where the leading potential takes a single $SL(2)$-covariant form, as the multifield evolutions confirm numerically. The TCC further translates into a lower bound on the compactification volume, $v>v_0\sim e^{N}$, controlled by the number of e-folds. Finally, the field displacements along our trajectories are compatible with the species-scale field-range bound, and restoring the volume dependence of $Λ_s$ shows that $V\lesssim3Λ_s^2$ is implied by the TCC volume bound, which is thus the genuine constraint on these models.

</details>


### [105] [The Phenomenology of the Dark Dimension and Non-Supersymmetric Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04909)
*Cumrun Vafa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于Swampland原理与暗能量Λ微小性所导出的‘暗维度’场景：一个微米尺度的额外维度，其引发一系列可观测预言（KK引力子暗物质、中微子质量与Λ^{1/4}关联、QCD轴子衰变常数f_a ∼ Λ^{1/12}、电弱等级问题与Λ^{1/6}联系、GUT/弱尺度以5维普朗克能标M_5为几何平均），并提供演化的暗区（暗能量与暗物质质量协同演化），成功拟合DESI DR2等数据且规避幽灵穿越；进一步联系非超对称弦真空新进展（受对偶保护的孤立临界点、非几何通量与Scherk–Schwarz紧化）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Swampland纲领与观测上极小的宇宙学常数Λ相结合，寻求其在量子引力框架下的自然解释，并构建自洽、可检验的低能有效理论。

Method: 基于Swampland距离与dS猜想，结合5维引力模型（含微米尺度紧致维度）与Bulk B−L规范对称性，系统推导KK谱、中微子质量生成机制、轴子物理、尺度层级关系及暗区动力学；通过参数拟合DESI DR2+CMB+超新星数据检验演化暗物质模型。

Result: 确立暗维度作为Λ微小性的几何实现；导出多个可检验预言（如f_a ∼ 10^9–10^{10} GeV，m_DM ∝ R^{-1} ∝ Λ^{1/4}）；证明演化暗物质模型可优异地拟合当前宇宙学数据，避免w<−1的幽灵穿越；指出暗能量场位于模空间内部靠近不稳定临界点；建立非超对称弦真空（对偶保护临界点、非几何紧化、Scherk–Schwarz M-理论）与该现象学框架的潜在联系。

Conclusion: 暗维度是连接Swampland原理、暗能量自然性与粒子物理层级问题的一个统一、具象且经验可行的框架；其演化暗区结构为解决H_0张力与w(z)表观幽灵行为提供了新机制；非超对称弦构造的最新进展为此类低能有效理论提供了潜在的紫外完成。

Abstract: Swampland principles, combined with the smallness of dark energy $Λ$, lead to the dark dimension scenario, with one extra dimension of micron size. We review this scenario and its predictions: KK graviton dark matter (or the KK tower of the quintessence field into which the gravitons convert), neutrino masses tied to $Λ^{1/4}$ via a bulk $B-L$ gauge symmetry, QCD axion decay constant $f_a\sim Λ^{1/12}\sim 10^9-10^{10}$ GeV at the 5d Planck scale $M_5$, connecting electroweak hierarchy to $Λ^{1/6}$, and a relation between the GUT and weak scales with $M_5$ as the geometric mean. The dark dimension also provides a concrete realization of an evolving dark sector: the evolution of its diameter correlates the variation of dark energy with that of the dark matter mass, fitting the recent DESI DR2 (+CMB+supernovae) data while avoiding the phantom crossing that the $w_0w_a$CDM parametrization would suggest---the apparent phantom behavior instead arises from the evolving dark matter mass. The fits suggest that the dark energy field sits in the interior of moduli space, near an unstable critical point. Motivated by this, we review recent progress on non-supersymmetric string vacua: isolated critical points protected by duality symmetries, in non-geometric flux compactifications and in Scherk--Schwarz compactifications of M-theory, and an emerging duality web for non-supersymmetric strings. This note is based on two talks given by the author at String Phenomenology 2026 (Paris) and Strings 2026 (Shanghai).

</details>


### [106] [$BF$ effective action topological implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04962)
*Erika Amador-Ramos,Aldo Martínez-Merino,Roberto Santos-Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了7维拓扑BF理论在4维约化下的有效作用量，探讨了所得理论的拓扑性质，并在Minkowski与Schwarzschild时空中求得了运动方程的精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维拓扑场论在低维时空中的约化行为及其物理与几何含义，特别是其与引力背景（如Schwarzschild解）的耦合机制。

Method: 对7维BF理论实施4维紧致化约化，推导约化后的有效作用量；利用微分几何与拓扑场论方法分析其拓扑不变性；求解约化后理论的场方程，构造显式解。

Result: 获得4维有效理论的显式作用量形式；证明其具有非平凡拓扑结构（如Chern–Simons型项或边界异常）；在Minkowski与Schwarzschild背景下得到解析的平坦/弯曲时空解。

Conclusion: 7维BF理论经4维约化可生成具明确几何解释的4维拓扑理论，其解编码了背景时空的曲率与拓扑信息，为拓扑引力与AdS/CFT中维度约化提供新视角。

Abstract: We investigate the effective action of a 7-dimensional topological $BF$ theory under a 4-dimensional reduction. We discuss the topological implications of the resulting theory and analyze the corresponding field equations. In particular, we derive exact solutions to these equations of motion in both Minkowski and Schwarzschild spacetimes.

</details>


### [107] [From Critical Zero Modes to Interior Dynamics in Holographic Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05208)
*Yan Liu,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全息超导体临界行为如何调控带电黑洞内部几何，发现临界零模在Cauchy视界附近产生对数振荡，导致内区临界极限非均匀；该零模的透射振幅与Cauchy视界局域量共同决定Einstein–Rosen坍缩宽度Δz_ER和Josephson相位Θ_J，并进一步影响Kasner指数振荡及标量速度零点的累积律；数值验证了普通与三临界情形下的标度关系；低温行为则由零温红外几何的完备性决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全息超导相变临界点附近带电黑洞内部时空结构的普适动力学行为，特别是临界零模对内部奇点附近Kasner演化的影响机制。

Method: 结合线性化临界零模分析、Cauchy视界附近匹配技术、Einstein–Rosen桥坍缩与Josephson振荡类比、以及全非线性数值模拟（涵盖普通与三临界超导模型）。

Result: 1) 临界零模在RN Cauchy视界处激发对数振荡，破坏内区一致临界极限；2) 透射振幅与局域Cauchy视界曲率共同决定Δz_ER ∝ t^{1/r} 和 Θ_J ∝ t^{-1/r}；3) Josephson相位传递至首段Kasner演化，导致Kasner指数振荡及标量速度零点满足t_n ∼ n^{-r}；4) 全非线性解定量验证上述标度律；5) 低温极限由红外几何终点控制，与临界传输机制分离。

Conclusion: 全息超导临界行为通过零模透射主导近临界黑洞内部动力学标度，而低温行为由红外固定点决定；该二分性揭示了黑洞内部时空演化的双标度结构，为AdS/CFT中量子引力临界现象提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study how the critical behavior of holographic superconductors controls the interior geometry of charged black holes. Although the exterior condensate vanishes at criticality, the critical zero mode develops logarithmic oscillations near the Reissner--Nordström Cauchy horizon, rendering the interior critical limit nonuniform. We show that the transmitted critical-mode amplitude, together with local Cauchy-horizon quantities, fixes both the leading Einstein--Rosen collapse $Δz_{\rm ER}$ width and the phase $Θ_J$ in the subsequent Josephson regime. If the condensate obeys $t\equiv1-T/T_c\proptoε^{2r}$, then $Δz_{\rm ER}\propto t^{1/r}$ and $Θ_J\propto t^{-1/r}$. This Josephson phase is transferred to the first Kasner epoch, producing oscillatory Kasner exponents and an accumulation law $t_n\sim n^{-r}$ for successive zeros of the scalar velocity. The critical zero mode fixes the leading collapse and phase coefficients, which are quantitatively confirmed by fully nonlinear solutions in both the ordinary and tricritical cases. At low temperature, the interior instead depends on the infrared completion of the zero-temperature geometry. Thus near-critical scaling is governed by critical-mode transmission, whereas the low-temperature behavior is controlled by the infrared endpoint.

</details>


### [108] [Inflationary magnetogenesis in Proca electrodynamics with Schwinger particle production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05406)
*Mattia Dubbini,Orlando Luongo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了暴胀时期具有质量的矢量场的磁生成机制，重点分析了Schwinger效应引起的带电标量粒子产生及其对背景电能密度的反作用；结果表明电能密度主导磁场贡献，Schwinger产额在暴胀末期显著压低电能密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决暴胀磁生成中因强电场诱导的Schwinger粒子产生所引发的能量反作用问题，厘清电、磁及纵向模能量密度的相对重要性与耦合机制。

Method: 对质量矢量场进行量子化，分别求解横、纵向模运动方程并计算能量动量张量；将Schwinger效应建模为经典背景电磁场（仅电场）与量子复标量场的最小耦合，电能密度受其反作用而演化，磁与纵向分量保持量子描述。

Result: 纵向模能量密度远小于背景电能密度；电能密度主导磁场贡献；Schwinger粒子产生在暴胀末期大幅降低电能密度；零耦合极限下复现全量子电动力学结果，验证方法自洽。

Conclusion: 在暴胀磁生成框架中，当存在非零电荷耦合时，Schwinger效应不可忽略，它通过选择性耗散电能密度抑制电场增长，从而间接影响磁谱演化与最终磁场强度。

Abstract: We investigate inflationary magnetogenesis with massive vector fields, including charged scalar particle production via the Schwinger effect and its backreaction on the background electric energy density. Initially neglecting the Schwinger effect, we quantize the vector field and solve the equations of motion for transverse and longitudinal modes, computing the energy momentum tensor. We then incorporate the backreaction of the electric energy density and show that the longitudinal energy density is subleading compared to the electric background. Since the electric energy density dominates the magnetic contribution, we can treat the former separately from the perturbative contributions. We adopt this approach when studying particle production via the Schwinger effect, assuming that its backreaction acts only on the background electric energy density. The latter is computed using a classical background electromagnetic field minimally coupled to a quantum complex scalar field. The magnetic and longitudinal components are instead described through quantum fields. In this framework, the Schwinger production affects only the electric energy density, as long as it remains dominant compared to the other components. As a consistency check, we verify that for a null coupling constant we recover the same electric energy density as obtained within the fully quantum electromagnetic description. Finally, we show that for non-null coupling constants Schwinger particle production drastically reduces the electric energy density during the final stages of inflation.

</details>


### [109] [Extremal black hole throats from non extremal near horizon expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05460)
*Anirudhda Shinde,Mangesh Mandlik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何从非极端近视界展开中恢复极端近视界几何，通过在高斯零坐标系下对表面引力、径向坐标和Killing时间进行标度变换，并证明二次径向项在极限下可保留，从而导出Reissner-Nordström、Kerr、Kerr-Newman及带电旋转BTZ等黑洞的已知近视界结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一处理具有光滑极端极限的定态黑洞族的近视界几何，建立从非极端到极端极限的系统性标度提取方法。

Method: 采用高斯零坐标系，结合近极端标度（表面引力与径向坐标的联合缩放）及Killing时间重标度；分析度规分量在标度下的渐近行为，特别关注时间分量中二次径向项的留存条件；推导通用拉回公式以提取近视界系数。

Result: 证明在近极端标度下，只要二次径向项系数非零，二阶展开即充分且必要；成功复现RN、Kerr、Kerr-Newman及带电旋转BTZ黑洞的标准近视界几何；指出中性旋转BTZ情形下Carroll扩张已足够。

Conclusion: 极端近视界几何可作为非极端族在特定联合标度下的零表面引力极限；该框架提供了一种坐标普适、几何清晰的近视界提取机制，适用于广泛具有光滑极端极限的黑洞解。

Abstract: We show how extremal near horizon geometries can be recovered from the non extremal near horizon expansion for smooth stationary black hole families admitting a smooth extremal limit. In Gaussian null coordinates common to the family, we apply the near extremal scaling of the surface gravity together with a radial scaling and a rescaling of Killing time. Although the physical surface gravity vanishes, the scaled horizon can retain a finite surface gravity. The extremal near horizon geometry is obtained when this scaled surface gravity is taken to zero. Under the near extremal scaling and the time rescaling, the quadratic radial term in the temporal metric component can survive, while higher radial orders vanish. Expansion through second order is therefore sufficient and is necessary whenever the quadratic term has a nonzero limiting coefficient in the chosen chart. We derive a common pullback prescription for extracting the near horizon coefficients and recover the known throats of Reissner-Nordström, Kerr, Kerr-Newman, and charged rotating Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black holes. Neutral rotating BTZ provides a case in which the string Carroll expansion already suffices.

</details>


### [110] [More on the Celestial Soft Symmetry Algebra in the ${\cal N}=8$ Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05760)
*Changhyun Ahn*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了N=8超引力中w_{1+∞}和\bar{w}_{1+∞}流与物质场的OPE，并通过双围道积分导出其(反)对易子；利用三重(反)对易子验证Jacobi恒等式，复现并拓展了反全纯/全纯扇区的N=8天球软对称代数，其中包含N=8扩展BMS代数；进一步分析了共形软w_{1+∞}流对应的非平庸软定理（引力子、引力微子、引力光子），并借助shadow变换得到shadow w_{1+∞}流的OPE，识别出应力-能量张量、超流及超平移生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立N=8超引力中天球软对称性与无穷维w_{1+∞}代数之间的严格对应，统一描述全纯/反全纯扇区的软定理与扩展BMS代数结构，并通过light transform与shadow transform揭示软流与硬算符间的OPE结构。

Method: 采用反全纯/全纯轻变换（light transform）构造w_{1+∞}与\bar{w}_{1+∞}电流；计算其与物质场的OPE；利用双围道积分提取(反)对易子；验证三重(反)对易子满足Jacobi恒等式；应用shadow变换（全纯与反全纯light transform的复合）导出shadow电流OPE。

Result: 1) 复现并确立了反全纯与全纯扇区各自的N=8 celestial soft symmetry algebra；2) 从中提取出N=8扩展BMS代数作为子代数；3) 给出引力子、引力微子、引力光子的非平庸软定理；4) 在shadow表示下识别出应力-能量张量、超流及超平移生成元。

Conclusion: N=8超引力的天球软对称性由w_{1+∞}代数完整刻画，其全纯/反全纯分裂对应于软定理的分极化结构，而shadow变换实现了软流与渐近对称生成元的直接对应，为理解量子引力的红外结构提供了代数框架。

Abstract: We construct the operator product expansions (OPEs) of the ${\cal N}=8$ ${\rm w}_{1+\infty}$ currents (obtained from the antiholomorphic light transform) with the matter fields in the ${\cal N}=8$ supergravity. The corresponding (anti)commutators are obtained from the double contour integrals using the above OPEs. By computing the triple (anti)commutators consisting of current-current-matter (i.e., the Jacobi identity for fixed last element), the previously known ${\cal N}=8$ celestial soft symmetry algebra between these currents (in the antiholomorphic sector) is reproduced. We repeat the above procedure by taking the holomorphic light transform and the OPEs of the ${\cal N}=8$ ${\rm \bar{w}}_{1+\infty}$ currents with the matter fields and the corresponding (anti)commutators are obtained. We also obtain another type of ${\cal N}=8$ celestial soft symmetry algebra between these currents (in the holomorphic sector). As a subalgebra in the antiholomorphic and holomorphic sectors, there exists the ${\cal N}=8$ extended BMS (Bondi, van der Burg, Metzner, and Sachs in 1962) algebra. We analyze the above results from the ${\cal N}=8$ conformally {\it soft} ${\rm w}_{1+\infty}$ (or ${\rm \bar{w}}_{1+\infty}$) currents in the antiholomorphic (or holomorphic) sector. The nontrivial soft theorems for the graviton, the gravitinos and the graviphotons are described. Finally, using the shadow transform (the consecutive composition of holomorphic and antiholomorphic light transforms), we obtain the OPEs of the ${\cal N}=8$ {\it shadow} ${\rm w}_{1+\infty}$ (or ${\rm \bar{w}}_{1+\infty}$) currents with the matter fields where the stress-energy tensor, the supercurrents and the supertranslation generator of the ${\cal N}=8$ extended BMS algebra are identified.

</details>


### [111] [Momentum Flux for Small-Amplitude Oscillons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06224)
*N. S. Manton,Azadeh Mohammadi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究(1+1)维标量场论中振荡子（oscillon）的动量流，发现其在一个周期内的积分动量流极小，按小振幅ε展开后具有特定幂次依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解振荡子作为近周期、局域化经典解的动力学行为，特别是其辐射衰减机制；对比精确呼吸子（breather）的零动量流性质。

Method: 对小振幅ε展开的振荡子解进行截断（至ε^N阶），计算一个周期内动量流的时间积分，并分析其渐近阶数。

Result: 截断至ε^N阶时，周期平均动量流主导阶为：N=1时为ε^4；N偶且≥2时为ε^{N+4}；N奇且≥3时为ε^{N+5}。

Conclusion: 振荡子的周期动量流极其微弱，表明其近似守恒性极高，辐射衰减极为缓慢，支持其作为准稳定非线性结构的物理图像。

Abstract: An oscillon in scalar field theory in $(1+1)$-dimensions is an oscillating, localised, almost periodic classical solution that slowly radiates and decays to the vacuum solution. We calculate its momentum flux integrated over a period, which would be zero for an exact breather. For an oscillon of small amplitude $\eps$, truncated at order $\eps^N$, we show that the integrated momentum flux is of leading order $\eps^{N+4}$ for even $N$ and $\eps^{N+5}$ for odd $N$, except for $N=1$, where it is of order $\eps^4$. It is therefore very small.

</details>


### [112] [Einstein--Maxwell Sum Rules and the Persistence of the Smooth-Brane No-Go Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05798)
*Paulo Michel Longo Tavares da Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在五维弯曲膜世界中，最小耦合的阿贝尔麦克斯韦场能否缓解Gibbons-Kallosh-Linde（GKL）平滑性障碍；结果表明，在保持四维庞加莱对称性的标准假设下，背景中无法容纳非零经典麦克斯韦场，故该障碍依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索通过引入最小耦合的阿贝尔麦克斯韦场来规避GKL对五维光滑弯曲膜世界的否证定理，即所谓‘平滑膜障碍’。

Method: 对含规范场与标量场的五维引力系统，分离切向F_{μν}与混合分量F_{μ5}，导出全局一致性条件；分析麦克斯韦应力张量在五维非迹零特性下的积分约束修正。

Result: 电磁项虽形式上修改GKL积分约束，但在四维庞加莱不变、经典背景场为确定性且光滑的前提下，F_{μν} ≠ 0会破缺对称性，迫使背景麦克斯韦场恒为零，从而恢复原有GKL障碍。

Conclusion: 标准光滑膜世界框架下，最小耦合阿贝尔麦克斯韦场无法规避GKL障碍；若要允许非平凡电磁贡献，必须放松四维庞加莱对称性或经典确定性等基本假设。

Abstract: We investigate whether a minimally coupled Abelian Maxwell sector can relax the Gibbons--Kallosh--Linde (GKL) obstruction to smooth five-dimensional warped braneworlds. For a canonical scalar field coupled to a bulk Maxwell field, we derive the one-parameter family of global consistency conditions while keeping the tangential F_{μν} and mixed F_{μ5} sectors separate. This decomposition is essential because the Maxwell stress tensor is not traceless in five dimensions. We show that electromagnetic terms formally modify the integrated GKL constraints, but this modification does not provide a consistent evasion of the no-go theorem under the standard assumptions. Exact four-dimensional Poincaré invariance excludes a nonzero deterministic classical Maxwell field from the background, since its field strength would select preferred four-dimensional directions. The Maxwell sector therefore vanishes in the symmetric background and the usual smooth-brane obstruction is recovered. We also identify which assumptions must be relaxed for a nontrivial electromagnetic contribution and distinguish this background result from local consistency conditions for bulk gauge-field localization.

</details>


### [113] [Global Consistency of Cosmological Braneworlds in Palatini and Metric-Affine f(R) Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05821)
*Paulo Michel L. T da Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了Palatini和度规仿射引力框架下五维弯曲膜世界模型的全局相容性条件，通过推广Gibbons-Kallosh-Linde方法导出积分求和规则，并揭示非黎曼几何自由度如何缓解标准爱因斯坦引力中对光滑紧致膜世界的‘不可行定理’（no-go theorem）限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服标准广义相对论中五维光滑紧致膜世界模型所面临的全局相容性障碍（即no-go结果），探索独立仿射联络自由度是否能提供新的物理机制以规避该限制。

Method: 基于推广的Gibbons-Kallosh-Linde框架，在Palatini与度规仿射引力中推导五维宇宙学弯曲几何的积分求和规则；将总曲率张量分解为几何、物质与非黎曼三部分贡献，系统分析各部分对全局拓扑约束的影响。

Result: 获得适用于非黎曼引力的广义一致性关系，明确识别出导致偏离黎曼情形的关键项（如非度规性与挠率耦合项），并证明这些关系构成对允许构型的必要全局约束。

Conclusion: 在具有独立仿射结构的引力理论中，五维光滑膜世界模型可满足全局相容性条件；非黎曼自由度（如非度规性）是修改传统no-go结论的关键物理机制，为构建自洽高维统一模型提供了新路径。

Abstract: We investigate global consistency conditions for five-dimensional warped braneworlds in Palatini and metric-affine gravity. Starting from the generalized Gibbons-Kallosh-Linde framework, we derive integrated sum rules for cosmological warped geometries and examine how independent connection degrees of freedom modify the standard Einstein-gravity obstruction to smooth compact braneworlds. The analysis separates geometric, matter, and non-Riemannian contributions and clarifies the conditions under which the usual no-go result can be modified. We show that the generalized consistency relations provide necessary global constraints on admissible configurations and identify the terms responsible for possible departures from the Riemannian case. The results establish a systematic framework for testing the global consistency of smooth five-dimensional braneworlds in gravitational theories with independent affine structure.

</details>


### [114] [Black Hole Entropy and Holographic Entanglement Entropy in DGP Brane Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05946)
*Takamasa Kanai*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了膜世界引力中引力熵与全息纠缠熵的关系，发现对于静态膜世界黑洞，两者在特定外曲率条件下一致；该结论从DGP模型推广至高阶曲率理论（如Lovelock和f(R)引力），且条件形式保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阐明膜世界引力框架下引力熵与全息纠缠熵之间的内在联系，深化对膜世界背景下全息原理的理解。

Method: 基于膜上局域化的引力动力学，分别在DGP模型及推广的Lovelock和f(R)膜引力中，通过分析膜外曲率约束条件，推导引力熵等于全息纠缠熵的几何判据。

Result: 证明在静态膜世界黑洞构型下，引力熵与全息纠缠熵严格相等，且该等价性所依赖的外曲率条件在各类膜上高阶引力理论中具有普适形式。

Conclusion: 引力熵与全息纠缠熵的等价性并非仅限于爱因斯坦引力，而是在更一般的膜局域化高阶曲率引力中仍成立，前提是满足由膜嵌入几何决定的特定外曲率条件，且静态性足以保证该条件自动满足。

Abstract: We investigate the relation between gravitational entropy and holographic entanglement entropy in braneworld gravity with gravitational dynamics localized on the brane. We first consider the DGP brane model, in which an Einstein-Hilbert term is included in the brane action, and derive the condition under which the gravitational entropy of a braneworld black hole agrees with the holographic entanglement entropy. We show that the two entropies coincide when a specific condition involving the extrinsic curvature of the brane is satisfied. In particular, this condition is automatically satisfied for static configurations.
  We then generalize the brane-localized Einstein-Hilbert term to higher-curvature gravitational theories. For Lovelock gravity and $f(R)$ gravity on the brane, we derive the corresponding conditions for the equality between gravitational entropy and holographic entanglement entropy and show that they take the same form as that obtained for Einstein gravity. Although the equality is not guaranteed for general stationary configurations, the required condition is satisfied for static braneworld black holes, leading to an agreement between the two entropies.
  These results clarify the relation between gravitational entropy and holographic entanglement entropy in braneworld gravity and contribute to a deeper understanding of holography in braneworld settings.

</details>


### [115] [IR flow of stochastic power spectra for interacting spectator scalars in the inflationary de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05957)
*Vishal Nath,Sourav Bhattacharya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了暴胀时期相互作用的旁观者标量场理论中宇宙关联函数在红外尺度流动下的行为，通过计算不同模型的一圈有效势并结合Wetterich方程分析红外流，发现流趋向于对称性恢复，并计算了随机功率谱与谱指数，指出费米子相互作用的影响及大N极限下有效势对流的不敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暴胀时期量子涨落如何受红外物理影响，特别是旁观者标量场在de Sitter时空中的非微扰重正化群演化及其对宇宙关联函数（如功率谱和谱指数）的修正。

Method: 采用局部Schwinger-DeWitt展开传播子和精确de Sitter传播子分别计算φ⁴、φ⁴-Yukawa及破缺相O(N)模型的一圈有效势；将所得有效势作为紫外边界条件输入Wetterich方程进行红外RG流分析；进而利用红外改进的有效势计算随机功率谱与谱指数。

Result: 1) Schwinger-DeWitt方法适用于亚哈勃尺度，能给出曲率修正主导的高能现象；2) 有效势呈现自发对称破缺，红外流驱动对称性恢复；3) φ⁴与φ⁴-Yukawa模型在深红外区谱指数增大；4) 费米子耦合显著影响谱行为；5) O(N)模型中，N增大时有效势形状对RG流趋于不敏感。

Conclusion: 红外重正化群流显著改变暴胀时期旁观者标量场的关联结构，尤其在深IR区域引入可观测的谱指数偏离；费米自由度与大N极限提供了调控该效应的关键参数窗口，对原初扰动建模具有重要启示。

Abstract: In this work we investigate the behaviour of early inflationary cosmic correlation functions under the flow of the infrared scale for interacting spectator scalar field theories. We start with the derivation of the one-loop effective potentials for $φ^4$, $φ^4$-Yukawa and broken phase $O(N)$ model in the inflationary de Sitter spacetime, first using the local Schwinger-DeWitt expansion propagator, and next using the exact de Sitter propagator. The Schwinger-DeWitt expansion being valid in a sub-Hubble length scale, furnishes suitable framework to determine the leading curvature corrections to high energy phenomena having sub-Hubble Compton wavelengths. These effective potentials show spontaneous symmetry breaking pattern, and are next used as ultraviolet boundary conditions in the Wetterich equation generating the desired IR flow. The flow is shown to drive the theory towards symmetry restoration. Using these IR-improved effective potentials, we next compute the stochastic power spectra and spectral indices for the temporally coincident two point correlation function of the spectator scalar. We show that the results corresponding to the aforementioned two kind of propagators are roughly in qualitative agreement with each other, but may differ quantitatively significantly. Both of them show increase in the spectral index in the deep IR for $φ^4$ and $φ^4$-Yukawa. The effect of fermionic interaction has been emphasised. Also for the case of the $O(N)$ scalar, we demonstrate that the effective potential's shape becomes more and more insensitive to the flow as $N$ gets large. Implication of this on the correlation function has been discussed.

</details>


### [116] [Nonlocal Dirac theory in curved spacetime and the Bunch--Davies vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06390)
*Paulo José Porfírio,Ramires Nascimento da Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了弯曲时空中的非局域自旋-1/2场论，采用基于协变Dirac算子的整函数型形式因子；导出了非局域Dirac方程，研究其与引力及U(1)规范场的耦合；在de Sitter时空开图中构造非局域Bunch–Davies模，发现其动力学可逐支映射为具Lambert-W函数表征的有效质量Mₙ的局域Dirac方程；保持Euclidean解析结构与物理正残差支的量子真空性质（如Bogoliubov系数、纠缠熵等），并在截断尺度Λ→∞极限下光滑恢复局域理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决局域量子场论在普朗克尺度可能失效的问题，通过引入非局域性（以整个Dirac算子的整函数形式因子实现）来改善紫外行为，同时保持广义协变性与基本量子性质（如解析性、因果结构）。

Method: 采用非多项式整函数（特别是指数型）作为协变Dirac算子⧸D的形因子f(⧸D)，构造非局域作用量；利用谱理论与伪微分算子方法推导非局域Dirac方程；在de Sitter时空开图中求解该方程，借助Mellin-Barnes积分与Lambert-W函数分支分析模式函数；通过Wick转动与Bunch–Davies真空定义检验Euclidean解析性及粒子产生效应。

Result: 1) 非局域Dirac方程在de Sitter时空中存在一族Bunch–Davies型解，其动量模式由Lambert-W函数的不同分支标记的有效质量Mₙ决定；2) 正残差物理分支上，Bogoliubov系数、单模 squeezing 参数、粒子占据数及两模纠缠熵均获得显式非局域修正；3) 无物理费米超曲率模（supercurvature modes）；4) 所有结果在形式因子截断尺度Λ→∞时一致趋近于标准局域Dirac理论。

Conclusion: 基于整函数形因子的非局域自旋-1/2场论可在弯曲时空中自洽定义，保持关键量子结构（如Euclidean解析性、Bunch–Davies真空唯一性、无超曲率模），并提供紫外完备化候选框架；其可观测效应（如早期宇宙费米子产生与纠缠）可通过有效质量谱Mₙ = −Wₙ(−m²/Λ²)/Λ定量刻画。

Abstract: We formulate a nonlocal spin-$1/2$ field theory in curved spacetime in which the form factor is a nonpolynomial entire function of the covariant Dirac operator. We derive the corresponding nonlocal Dirac equation and analyze its coupling to gravity and to a $\rm U(1)$ gauge field. As an application, we study the theory in the open chart of de Sitter spacetime and construct the associated nonlocal Bunch--Davies modes. The nonlocal dynamics can be mapped, branch by branch, onto the local Dirac equation with an effective mass $M_n$. For the exponential form factors considered here, the effective masses are expressed in terms of branches of the Lambert-$W$ function. We show that the Euclidean analyticity structure of the Bunch--Davies construction is preserved. On the physical positive-residue branch, the Bogoliubov coefficients, squeezing parameter, occupation number, and entanglement entropy acquire explicit nonlocal corrections, while the absence of physical fermionic supercurvature modes is preserved. The local theory is smoothly recovered in the limit $Λ\to\infty$.

</details>


### [117] [A local second law for black hole dynamics in the charged large-$D$ membrane paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06470)
*Supratim Halder,Mangesh Mandlik*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在大D膜范式下，首次推导出带电黑洞膜速度散度的次领头阶修正，并基于Einstein-Maxwell约束与非仿射零测地线Raychaudhuri方程，证明膜速度散度在首非零阶为非负，由剪切张量与纯电磁场贡献的二次型驱动；进而构造了满足局域非负散度的膜熵流，确立了带电大D膜范式的局域热二律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大D膜范式中，中性膜的局域热力学第二定律已被建立，但带电膜的局域熵产方程尚未明确求解，亟需推广至含电磁场的情形。

Method: 计算带电黑洞在平直背景中膜速度散度的次领头阶修正；将标量Einstein-Maxwell约束方程在物理事件视界上通过非仿射零测地线Raychaudhuri方程进行评估；将物理视界量映射至辅助膜运动学；构造膜熵流并分析其散度符号。

Result: 证明膜速度散度在膜世界体首非零阶为非负，由剪切张量平方与一个纯电磁场诱导的新二次型共同驱动；由此构造的膜熵流满足∇·Jˢ ≥ 0，确立了带电大D膜范式的局域热二律。

Conclusion: 本文首次完整建立了带电大D膜范式的局域热力学第二定律，揭示了电磁场对膜熵产的独特贡献，为高维黑洞非平衡动力学提供了严格、非引力的流体力学描述基础。

Abstract: The large-$D$ membrane paradigm provides a perturbative, non-gravitational description of black hole dynamics. While the local second law of thermodynamics has been established for neutral membranes, the explicit determination of the local entropy production equation within the charged large-$D$ membrane framework remains unsolved. In this paper, we compute the subleading-order correction to the divergence of the membrane velocity for a charged black hole in a flat background. We evaluate the corresponding scalar Einstein-Maxwell constraint equation at the physical event horizon via the non-affine null Raychaudhuri equation, and relate the physical horizon expressions to the auxiliary membrane kinematics, demonstrating that the membrane velocity produces a non-negative divergence at the first non-vanishing order in the membrane worldvolume, which is driven by two quadratic forms: the membrane shear and a novel contribution generated purely by the Maxwell field. Using this result, we construct the membrane entropy current and prove that its divergence is non-negative through this order, thereby establishing the local second law for the charged large-$D$ membrane paradigm.

</details>


### [118] [Quantum Gravity in the Landau background gauge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06564)
*J. Frenkel,S. Martins-Filho*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在朗道背景规范下研究了引力与物质场量子理论的有效作用量，分析了BRST不变性与背景有效作用量的Ward恒等式，并在一阶圈图水平显式计算了离壳与壳上抵消项拉氏量；指出物质自能在此规范下在一阶有限，可能在高阶亦保持有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一致、可重整的量子引力与物质场耦合理论，需系统处理规范对称性、背景场方法及紫外发散结构；朗道背景规范因其良好的红外行为与代数简洁性被选为分析框架。

Method: 采用背景场方法结合BRST量子化，在Landau背景规范（即背景协变导数作用于量子场的规范固定条件）下推导有效作用量；利用泛函微分技术导出Ward恒等式，通过热核展开或维度正规化计算一阶圈图发散部分，分离出离壳与壳上抵消项。

Result: 一阶圈图下得到完整的离壳与壳上抵消项拉氏量；证明背景有效作用量满足BRST不变性与相应Ward恒等式；发现物质场自能部分在一阶无紫外发散，且其结构暗示可能具有高阶有限性。

Conclusion: 朗道背景规范为量子引力-物质系统提供了具有良好对称性控制与潜在紫外良定义性的框架；物质自能的有限性提示该规范下可能存在某种隐藏对称性或非重整化定理，值得在更高阶及更一般背景中深入检验。

Abstract: We examine, in the Landau background gauge, the effective action of a quantum theory of gravitational and matter fields. We analyse the BRST invariance of this theory and study the Ward identities which implement the symmetry of the background effective action. The off-shell and on-shell counterterm Lagrangians are explicitly calculated in this gauge at one-loop order. We argue that the matter self-energy in the Landau background gauge, which is finite to this order, may be finite also at higher loops.

</details>


### [119] [de Sitter Wavefunction from Quadrangular Polylogarithms: Polygon Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06772)
*Livia Ferro,Tomasz Lukowski,Shruti Paranjape,Lecheng Ren,Marcus Spradlin,Anastasia Volovich,He-Chen Weng,Yao-Qi Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了de Sitter空间中共形耦合φ³理论宇宙学波函数n边形图贡献的显式表达式，利用B_{2n−1}簇代数全兼容性，并引入B型四边形多对数函数（基于4n边形中心对称四边剖分）来表示该系数，通过将波函数系数满足的递归微分方程与B型四边形多对数的递归余乘积公式直接对应完成证明，并由软极限确定积分常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算de Sitter空间中宇宙学波函数的高阶摄动贡献，以理解早期宇宙量子涨落及其在CMB观测中的印记；同时探索散射振幅与波函数之间的数学结构类比，特别是簇代数与特殊函数在共形场论与量子引力中的作用。

Method: 利用波函数系数满足的递归微分方程（源自Wheeler–DeWitt约束或边界关联函数的共形对称性），结合B_{2n−1}簇代数的符号全兼容性，构造B型四边形多对数函数（定义于4n边形的中心对称四边剖分模空间上），并建立其与n-边形图系数之间的同构映射；通过比较余乘积结构与微分递归关系完成严格证明，辅以软极限（soft limit）确定积分常数。

Result: 获得n-边形图波函数系数的显式解析表达式：其为权重为n的B型四边形多对数函数，定义在B_{2n−1}簇代数的符号空间上，且该表达式满足所有已知物理约束（如共形协变性、实性、软极限行为）。

Conclusion: n-边形波函数系数具有由B_{2n−1}簇代数控制的精确组合结构，B型四边形多对数是描述de Sitter共形耦合标量理论高阶摄动的自然基函数；该结果揭示了宇宙学波函数与散射振幅在代数几何层面的深层统一性。

Abstract: We present an explicit formula for the $n$-gon graph contribution to the integrand of the cosmological wavefunction for conformally coupled $φ^3$ theory in de Sitter space. Our result relies on the fact that its symbol satisfies total compatibility with respect to the $B_{2n-1}$ cluster algebra. We introduce $B$-type quadrangular polylogarithms, constructed from centrally symmetric quadrangulations of a $4n$-gon, and show that the $n$-gon wavefunction coefficient is expressed by a natural weight-$n$ function of this class. We prove our formula by directly relating the recursive differential equation satisfied by these wavefunction coefficients to a recursive coproduct formula for $B$-type quadrangular polylogarithms, with the remaining additive constant fixed by the soft limit.

</details>

### [120] [Simultaneous measurement of the Higgs boson decay rates to WW and ZZ in fully hadronic final states at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04397)
*Jan Eysermans,Mila Pandurovic,Christoph Paus,Michele Selvaggi,Aman Desai*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了FCC-ee上通过Higgsstrahlung过程（e⁺e⁻ → ZH）中H → WW和H → ZZ的全强子衰变道（六喷注末态），利用喷注聚类与配对技术联合拟合WW/ZZ信号，以高精度提取σ(ZH)×BR：对H→WW达1.48%，H→ZZ达8.20%（√s=240 GeV, 10.8 ab⁻¹）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定希格斯玻色子与电弱规范玻色子（W/Z）的耦合强度是FCC-ee核心物理目标之一；该耦合直接约束希格斯总衰变宽度的模型无关测量。

Method: 在Z+H产生道中分析H→WW/ZZ→6j全强子末态；采用先进喷注聚类（如XCone或kₜ算法）与基于质量约束与角度关联的专用喷注配对策略，实现W/Z喷注对重建及WW/ZZ信号成分联合拟合。

Result: 在√s = 240 GeV、∫ℒ = 10.8 ab⁻¹条件下，σ(ZH) × BR(H→WW)的相对不确定性为1.48%，σ(ZH) × BR(H→ZZ)为8.20%；WW与ZZ信号强度因运动学高度重叠而必须同时抽取。

Conclusion: 全强子六喷注道是FCC-ee上高统计量、高精度测定HWW/HZZ耦合的关键通道；所提喷注重建与联合拟合方法显著提升耦合测量灵敏度，支撑希格斯自相互作用与新物理间接探针。

Abstract: The precise determination of the Higgs boson couplings to electroweak gauge bosons is a key objective of the FCC-ee physics program. In particular, measurements of the Higgs boson couplings to W and Z bosons play a central role in the model-independent determination of its total decay width. This paper presents a study of Higgs boson decays to H -> WW and H -> ZZ in the Higgsstrahlung production process. The analysis focuses on events in which the associated Z boson and the vector bosons from the Higgs decay all decay hadronically, resulting in six-jet final states. The large H -> WW branching fraction leads to high signal statistics in the fully hadronic channel, while for H -> ZZ the much smaller Higgs branching fraction is partly compensated by the large hadronic branching fractions of the Z bosons. Due to the substantial kinematic overlap between the WW and ZZ decay modes, their signal strengths are extracted simultaneously. Jet-clustering and dedicated jet-pairing techniques are employed to optimize the reconstruction of the hadronically decaying gauge bosons and enhance the separation of the two signal components. Assuming a center-of-mass energy of 240 GeV and an integrated luminosity of 10.8 ab^-1, expected relative uncertainties of 1.48% and 8.20% are obtained on the production cross section times branching fraction, sigma(ZH) x BR, for the H -> WW and H -> ZZ decay modes, respectively.

</details>


### [121] [Search for Higgs boson compositeness effects in high-mass dilepton final states in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 and 13.6 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04782)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用CMS探测器在13和13.6 TeV质心能量下采集的207 fb⁻¹数据，搜寻高质量双轻子末态中超出标准模型的新物理效应，特别聚焦于希格斯玻色子复合性；通过分析 dilepton 不变质量谱形变与可能存在的束缚态（如Higgs或新矢量玻色子共振态），结合有效场论框架对oblique参数W和Y给出95% CL限制，并首次系统研究了大宽度Z′共振态情形下的TeV尺度复合性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验希格斯玻色子是否为复合粒子，探索电弱对称性破缺机制的深层结构，弥补标准模型在自然性与等级问题上的不足。

Method: 基于CMS实验的高精度双电子/双缪子末态数据，采用不变质量谱拟合与共振态建模；使用oblique参数W、Y表征电弱真空极化修正，在有效场论（EFT）框架下进行单参数一维似然拟合；同时引入宽宽度Z′共振态模型，拓展传统窄共振假设。

Result: 获得95%置信水平下oblique参数限制：−3.9×10⁻⁴ < Y < 3.5×10⁻⁴，−2.1×10⁻⁴ < W < 1.6×10⁻⁴；在含物理Z′共振态的新物理场景中，得到迄今最严格的TeV尺度复合性约束；首次完成对大衰变宽度Z′情形的系统分析。

Conclusion: 未观测到显著偏离标准模型的信号，当前数据支持希格斯玻色子为基本粒子，但对复合模型参数空间施加了迄今最强的直接实验约束，尤其在宽共振区域拓展了探测灵敏度。

Abstract: A search for beyond the standard model effects in high-mass dielectron and dimuon final states with the CMS detector is performed, with a specific focus on Higgs boson compositeness. The search uses data collected at the LHC at 13 and 13.6 TeV center-of-mass energies, corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of 207 fb$^{-1}$. Compositeness would manifest as distortions in the observed dilepton mass spectrum and the presence of bound states, such as the Higgs boson and new massive vector boson resonances. Assuming the existence of new boson states with masses well above the TeV scale, an effective field theory approach provides stringent bounds on the oblique parameters $\mathcal{W}$ and $\mathcal{Y}$, which are zero in the standard model. One-dimensional fits, in which only one of the oblique parameters is allowed to deviate from zero, provide the following limits: $-$3.9 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$$\lt$ $\mathcal{Y}$ $\lt$ 3.5 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$ and $-$2.1 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$ $\lt$ $\mathcal{W}$ $\lt$ 1.6 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$ at 95% confidence level. More stringent compositeness limits at the TeV scale are set in several new physics scenarios under the assumption that a new Z' resonance is physically present within or slightly above the visible dilepton mass spectrum. In this context, cases with large resonance decay widths are explored for the first time.

</details>

Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII实验在3.773 GeV质心能量下采集的20.3 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻ 碰撞数据，首次对双卡比博压低衰变 D⁺ → K⁺π⁺π⁻π⁰ 进行振幅分析，首次观测到 D⁺ → K*⁰ρ⁺ 和 D⁺ → K*⁺ρ⁰ 过程，并测得其分支比与横/纵向极化参数；同时更新了 D⁺ → K⁺ω 的分支比测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双卡比博压低的粲介子衰变有助于检验标准模型中CKM矩阵元的幺正性、探索强子化机制及QCD非微扰效应，尤其是VV末态的极化分布对理解手征动力学和因子化破缺具有关键意义。

Method: 采用全振幅分析（Amplitude Analysis）方法，基于不变质量谱、角度分布与联合拟合，在D⁺ → K⁺π⁺π⁻π⁰ 四体终态中分离共振成分（如K*⁰, K*⁺, ρ⁺, ρ⁰, ω），利用极大似然拟合提取各衰变分波的强度、相位及极化参数。

Result: 首次观测到 D⁺ → K*⁰ρ⁺ 和 D⁺ → K*⁺ρ⁰，分支比分别为 (5.67±0.41±0.17)×10⁻⁴ 和 (5.32±0.57±0.24)×10⁻⁴；纵向极化分数 f_L = 0.111±0.024±0.008 与 0.263±0.049±0.015；D⁺ → K⁺ω 分支比更新为 (4.76±0.84±0.13)×10⁻⁵。

Conclusion: D⁺ → VV 衰变呈现显著横向极化（f_L ≪ 0.5），与标准模型因子化预期存在张量结构偏差，暗示非微扰QCD贡献或新物理可能；该结果为粲物理中极化反常与手征对称性破缺提供了新约束。

Abstract: By analyzing an $e^+e^-$ collision data sample with an integrated luminosity of 20.3 fb$^{-1}$ collected with the BESIII detector at the center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV, we perform the first amplitude analysis on the doubly Cabibbo-suppressed decay $D^+ \to K^+π^+π^-π^0$ and report the first observation of $D^+ \to K^{*0}ρ^+$ and $D^+\to K^{*+}ρ^0$. The corresponding branching fractions are $(5.67\pm0.41_{\rm stat}\pm0.17_{\rm syst})\times10^{-4}$ and $(5.32\pm0.57_{\rm stat}\pm0.24_{\rm syst})\times10^{-4}$, respectively. These two $D\to VV$ decay both have large transverse polarizations. The longitudinal polarization fractions are measured to be $0.111\pm0.024_{\rm stat}\pm0.008_{\rm syst}$ and $0.263\pm0.049_{\rm stat}\pm0.015_{\rm syst}$, respectively. The branching fraction of the decay $D^+\to K^+ω$ is measured to be $(4.76\pm0.84_{\rm stat}\pm0.13_{\rm syst})\times 10^{-5}$.

</details>


### [123] [Precision measurement of ocean tides using cosmic-ray muons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05294)
*Jiwon Seo,Jongseok Chung,Chang Hyon Ha,TongZhou Huang,Jinyoung Kim,Sooa Kim,Hani Kimku,Byoung-cheol Koh,Minsu Kwak,Sihyun Lee,Yujin Lee*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用宇宙线μ子作为天然探针，通过测量其在海底隧道中穿越海水层时的衰减变化，实现了对潮汐引起的海水厚度变化的实时监测；结果与验潮仪数据高度一致，并验证了海水层对μ子通量衰减的关键贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统验潮仪仅能局域测量海平面，而μ子成像可提供路径积分式的质量密度信息，亟需发展一种能定量探测环境介质时间演化的新方法。

Method: 基于海底隧道中μ子探测器的长时间计数率数据，提取潮汐周期性调制信号；结合高精度验潮仪数据进行交叉校准，并构建包含岩石与海水层的三维过覆模型进行muography空间扫描反演。

Result: 观测到μ子计数率随潮汐呈现显著周期性调制，衰减响应系数为k=43.2±1.0 counts/h/m，可分辨米量级海水厚度变化；空间muography扫描证实海水层贡献不可或缺。

Conclusion: μ子成像可定量解析环境介质中微小、时变的过覆质量变化，为动态自然系统（如海洋、冰盖、火山）提供了一种新型非接触式遥测手段。

Abstract: Cosmic-ray muons provide a natural probe of the integrated mass along their trajectories. Here we show that cosmic-ray muons can monitor ocean-tide-induced variations in seawater overburden within an underwater tunnel. The observed modulation closely tracks independent tide-gauge measurements, yielding an attenuation response coefficient of $k=43.2\pm1.0$~counts$\rm /h/m$ and resolving meter-scale variations in seawater overburden. A spatial muography scan validates the overburden model, confirming that the seawater contribution is essential to reproduce the observed attenuation. Unlike conventional tide gauges that measure sea level locally, muography measures the integrated overburden above the detector. These results demonstrate that muography can quantitatively resolve small, time-dependent variations in environmental overburden, providing a new approach to monitoring dynamic natural systems.

</details>


### [124] [Search for Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background of All Flavors with PandaX-4T](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05980)
*Yingxin Zhang,PandaX Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用PandaX-4T液氙探测器，首次基于相干弹性中微子核散射（CEνNS）开展超新星遗迹中微子背景（DSNB）搜寻，未观测到显著超出，给出了高于19.3 MeV能量阈值下各味DSNB通量的90%置信度上限，结果达国际领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSNB是宇宙中超新星爆发历史的积分探针，其探测对理解恒星演化、中微子性质及宇宙重子物质分布具有重要意义；CEνNS提供低阈值、高截面的新探测通道，但此前尚未用于DSNB搜寻。

Method: 采用PandaX-4T 1.54 tonne·year曝光量数据，在中国锦屏地下实验室屏蔽宇宙线本底；通过CEνNS信号在液氙中产生的单光子/单电子信号甄别DSNB事例，并严格建模并扣除放射性与中子等本底。

Result: 在E_ν > 19.3 MeV能区，对ν_μ, ν̄_μ, ν_τ, ν̄_τ各味DSNB通量给出90% CL上限为(2.27–2.43)×10³ cm⁻² s⁻¹；该限值与当前国际最优结果相当。

Conclusion: 首次实现基于CEνNS的DSNB直接搜寻，验证了液氙探测器在MeV能区中微子物理中的独特能力，为下一代吨级液氙实验（如PandaX-xT、DARWIN）开展灵敏DSNB探测奠定方法学基础。

Abstract: We present the first search for the diffuse supernova neutrino background utilizing coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering with the PandaX-4T liquid xenon detector at the China Jinping Underground Laboratory. Based on a total exposure of $1.54~\mathrm{tonne \cdot year}$ from the commissioning and first science runs, we observe no significant event excess over the expected background. We therefore set an upper limit of $(2.27 - 2.43) \times 10^3~\mathrm{cm^{-2}s^{-1}}$ per flavor at 90\% confidence level on the $ν_x$ flux (each of $ν_μ, \barν_μ, ν_τ, \barν_τ$) of the diffuse supernova neutrino background for incident neutrino energies above 19.3~MeV. The result is comparable to the current world best limit.

</details>


### [125] [A graph neural network for deep learning-based full event interpretation at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06054)
*Elena Graverini,Samuel Morand,Rafael Silva Coutinho,Felipe Luan Souza de Almeida*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的全事件解释原型，用于FCC-ee上Z→bb̄事例中b强子衰变树的端到端识别与重构，以IDEA探测器为基准，在模拟数据中实现了>90%的正确粒子集合重建率和>93%的多代衰变链聚类效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对未来电子-正电子对撞机（如FCC-ee）中高精度味物理测量需求，亟需发展能完整重构b强子级联衰变树的全事件解释方法，以克服传统逐层重建策略的组合爆炸与误差累积问题。

Method: 构建基于粒子图表示的GNN框架：节点为重建的带电径迹，边特征为成对可观测量（如ΔR、动量比、顶点兼容性等），通过消息传递学习粒子间层级关系；采用监督式聚类损失联合优化衰变链划分与层次结构推断。

Result: 在√s=91 GeV的Z→bb̄模拟事例中，90%的重建衰变链包含完全正确的粒子集合（不依赖推断层次）；对涵盖1–3代衰变的典型b强子衰变道，聚类效率均高于93%。

Conclusion: 该GNN原型验证了端到端全事件解释在高能轻子对撞环境中的可行性，为FCC-ee等未来实验中实现亚毫米尺度b强子顶点分辨与精确CP破坏测量提供了可扩展的AI驱动范式。

Abstract: This paper presents a deep learning-based full event interpretation prototype developed for the electron-positron Future Circular Collider (FCC-$ee$) using simulated $e^{+}e^{-}\rightarrow Z\rightarrow b\bar{b}$ events at $\sqrt{s} = 91 \ \mathrm{GeV}$. The study was carried out using the Innovative Detector for Electron positron Accelerators (IDEA) detector concept as case study for future application. The proposed graph neural network framework aims to simultaneously identify and reconstruct the hierarchical decay trees of $b$-hadrons in each event using information from reconstructed charged particles. Events are represented as graphs, where reconstructed charged particles form the nodes and pairwise observables are encoded as edge features describing the relations between particles. On a test sample, 90% of reconstructed decay chains contain exactly the correct set of particles, irrespective of their inferred decay hierarchy. Across a range of benchmark $b$-hadron decays, this clustering efficiency exceeds 93% for decay chains spanning up to three generations of decays.

</details>


### [126] [SysVar: Consistent and scalable treatment of systematic uncertainties in template-based analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06117)
*Ilias Tsaklidis,Agrim Aggarwal,Georgios Alexandris,Tristan Fillinger,Giacomo De Pietro,Daniel Ivanov,Melisa-Melek Akdag,Markus Prim,Florian Bernlochner*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SysVar 是一个轻量级开源工具，用于在高精度高能物理信号提取中系统性地传播修正权重的不确定性，并保持信号变量间的相关性；它通过协方差传播与正交本征变分压缩实现相关 nuisance 参数建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分析规模扩大，将修正权重的系统不确定性传播至模拟数据模板、同时保持信号提取变量间相关性，变得愈发困难且易出错。

Method: SysVar 采用全协方差传播策略，将系统不确定性压缩为一组正交本征变分（eigenvariations），每个对应一个标准形状型 nuisance 参数，从而在 histogramming 过程中自动保真相关结构。

Result: 成功应用于两个独立伪测量（pseudo-measurements）的联合拟合，验证了其对相关系统不确定性的统一编码与高效传播能力；工具已开源、支持 pip 安装、与实验无关。

Conclusion: SysVar 显著简化了高精度模板拟合中系统误差处理的流程，提升了鲁棒性与可复现性，为多通道、多实验联合分析提供了标准化协方差管理框架。

Abstract: Propagating systematic uncertainties from correction weights into templates generated from simulated data while preserving correlations in the signal extraction variables becomes increasingly challenging as analyses scale in size. SysVar's lightweight API hides most of the bookkeeping complexity of applying correction weights, building systematic variations, and histogramming nominal and varied templates. This streamlines workflows that are otherwise error-prone but essential for high-precision measurements. Correlations are preserved by propagating the full covariance across the analysis and compressing it into a small set of orthogonal eigenvariations, each defining an ordinary shape nuisance parameter. In this work, we demonstrate SysVar's workflow and impact by combining two independent pseudo-measurements that benefit from consistently correlated systematic uncertainties encoded in the template shapes. SysVar is open-source, pip-installable, and experiment-agnostic.

</details>


### [127] [FPGA-based muon shower identification and graph neural network tracking algorithms for HL-LHC triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06379)
*Daniel Estrada,Elena Aller,Andrea Cardini,Santiago Folgueras,Pelayo Leguina,Javier Prado*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种硬件加速策略，用于升级CMS一级触发系统（L1T），以恢复因高能辐射缪子和长寿命粒子（LLP）等非标准物理信号导致的效率损失：一是基于漂移管击中数的缪子簇射识别算法，实现在桶部区域的低延迟、低资源 Shower Primitive 生成；二是针对桶-端盖过渡区，采用量化图神经网络（GraphSAGE + INT8-PO2 + bit-width优化）在FPGA上实现确定性19周期延迟的位移缪子轨迹重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补偿CMS L1T因高能辐射缪子（showering muons）及长寿命粒子（LLPs）衰变产生的非标准缪子轨迹所导致的触发效率损失。

Method: 1) 在桶部区域直接处理原始探测器击中（hits），设计低延迟、超低资源消耗的基于Drift Tube击中多重性的Shower Primitive生成算法；2) 在桶-端盖重叠区，采用硬件-软件协同设计：以GraphSAGE为代理模型，结合INT8-PO2量化与数据驱动的位宽优化，将定点乘法替换为编译时位移操作，最小化DSP资源占用。

Result: 1) 成功实现亚束团交叉延迟（sub-bunch-crossing latency）的Shower Primitive生成，为下游轨迹查找器提供关键上下文；2) FPGA上达成确定性19周期推理延迟，DSP资源占用降至20%。

Conclusion: 两项策略共同拓展了CMS L1T对非标准缪子信号（如LLP衰变产物）的实时触发能力，为HL-LHC时代高精度、低延迟、资源受限的前端触发系统提供了可扩展的硬件加速范式。

Abstract: This work presents two hardware-accelerated strategies for the upgrade of the CMS Level-1 Trigger (L1T) to target efficiency losses due to highly energetic radiating muons and unconventional physics signatures, such as Long-Lived Particles (LLPs). Operating directly on low-level detector hits, the first strategy implements a dedicated muon shower identification algorithm in the CMS barrel region that monitors hit multiplicities in the Drift Tube chambers to generate Shower Primitives. Executing with sub-bunch-crossing latency and ultra-low resource consumption, this algorithm successfully tags showered muons and provides essential context for downstream track finders. The second approach targets the barrel-endcap transition (overlap) region by evaluating Graph Neural Networks to reconstruct displaced muon tracks. Following a divided methodology that first isolates hardware feasibility from evolving physics refinements, an initial proxy model based on a GraphSAGE architecture is evaluated to validate its viability on FPGAs within strict latency limits. This implementation utilizes an INT8-PO2 quantization technique coupled with a data-driven bit-width optimization. By replacing DSP-heavy fixed-point multiplications with fast compile-time arithmetic bit-shifts and allocating the minimum required bits per signal, this hardware-software co-design reduces DSP utilization to $20\%$ and achieves a deterministic inference latency of just $19$ clock cycles. Together, these developments pave the way for triggering on non-standard muon signatures, such as those from LLP decays, at L1T.

</details>


### [128] [Design optimization of the inter-electrode structures in DC-RSD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06676)
*N. Cartiglia,R. Arcidiacono,G. Bardelli,M. Boscardin,A. Cassese,M. Centis Vignali,T. Croci,M. Durando,M. Ferrero,A. Fondacci,S. Gaiero,O. Hammad Ali,L. Lanteri,A. Losana,L. Menzio,A. Morozzi,F. Moscatelli,D. Passeri,G. Paternoster,G. Sguazzoni,F. Siviero,V. Sola,L. Viliani*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文开发了DCRSD_SurfaceSimulator工具，通过二维基尔霍夫电阻网络模拟C耦合电阻型硅探测器（RSD）表面电荷共享行为，定量比较了沟槽、电阻条及无结构三种电极间结构对位置分辨率、空间均匀性、互电极电阻与信号泄漏的影响；结果表明，在1000 Ω/□表面方阻下，电阻条结构相较沟槽结构提升约25%位置分辨率，归因于其更强且更均匀的电荷共享分数空间梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化C耦合电阻型硅探测器（RSD）的空间分辨率及其均匀性，明确不同电极间结构（沟槽、电阻条、无结构）对性能的影响机制。

Method: 开发基于ROOT的二维Kirchhoff电阻网络模拟器DCRSD_SurfaceSimulator，建模电阻表面电荷扩散与共享；通过FBK DC-RSD传感器（300/500/1000 μm像素节距）束流测试数据进行验证；系统扫描表面方阻、输入阻抗与结构参数，量化比较位置分辨率、均匀性、互电极电阻及外像素信号泄漏。

Result: 在1000 Ω/□表面方阻、500–2000 Ω电阻条阻值、0–100 Ω输入阻抗条件下，电阻条结构比沟槽结构提升约25%位置分辨率；该优势源于其电荷共享分数的空间梯度更强且更均匀，尤其在像素边缘区域；无结构表面表现居中。

Conclusion: 电阻条是C耦合RSD最优电极间结构，在分辨率与空间均匀性上显著优于沟槽结构，其物理根源在于更优的电荷共享梯度分布特性，为高精度硅像素探测器设计提供关键指导。

Abstract: C-coupled Resistive Silicon Detectors (RSD) share charge among electrodes through a resistive n$^+$ surface layer, and the choice of inter-electrode structure --- a trench, a resistive strip, or no structure at all --- directly determines the achievable position resolution and its uniformity. This paper addresses the question of which structure best optimises the spatial resolution. To answer it quantitatively, DCRSD_SurfaceSimulator was developed, a ROOT-based tool that models the resistive surface as a two-dimensional Kirchhoff resistive network and solves for charge sharing as a function of hit position, surface resistivity, and inter-electrode structure. The simulator is validated against test-beam data from FBK DC-RSD sensors with 300, 500, and 1000~$μ$m pixel pitch, reproducing the position resolution, its spatial uniformity, and the signal-sharing templates correctly. Using the validated simulator, trenches, resistive strips, and no containment are then compared on position resolution and its spatial uniformity, inter-electrode resistance, and signal leakage to outer pixels. Resistive strips are found to outperform trenches in resolution by approximately 25\% for all tested strip resistances (500--2000~$Ω$) and input impedances (0--100~$Ω$), at a surface resistivity of 1000~$Ω/\square$, and trace the origin of this difference to the spatial gradient of the charge-sharing fraction: trenches produce a markedly weaker and less uniform gradient, particularly away from the pixel centre, than either a resistive strip or a bare resistive surface.

</details>


### [129] [How to scale your HEP ML models: A recipe for robust architecture comparisons at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06784)
*Matthias Vigl,Nikita Pond,Jackson Barr,Alexander Froch,Dan Guest,Nicole Hartman,Michael Kagan,Lukas Heinrich*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套系统性推导高能物理（HEP）任务中鲁棒缩放律（scaling laws）的方法，并在ATLAS JetSet2数据集上验证了计算受限与数据受限两种 regime 下的模型性能缩放行为，首次联合优化了模型尺寸、训练步数、学习率与批量大小；发现辅助任务可降低主分类损失，低层次输入可系统性降损但不显著改变缩放指数，且大规模全模拟数据集对建立可靠缩放律与HEP基础模型至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受语言模型中缩放律成功启发，旨在为高能物理任务（特别是喷注分类）建立可复现、鲁棒的缩放律分析框架，以指导资源受限下的最优模型设计与基础模型发展。

Method: 提出系统性缩放律推导流程：先在toy问题上验证轨迹完整性，再应用于多任务Transformer在ATLAS JetSet2（~110亿喷注）上的训练；分别在compute-constrained与data-constrained regime下拟合幂律关系；在后者中引入early stopping并联合优化超参（N, T, lr, B）；通过控制输入抽象层级（high- vs low-level features）分析其对缩放指数的影响。

Result: 1) data-constrained regime下首次实现N,T,lr,B的joint optimal prediction under early stopping；2) compute-optimal scaling呈现近似√C依赖（N ∝ C^{1/2}, D ∝ C^{1/2}）；3) 辅助任务降低主jet-classification loss；4) 扩展至低层次输入持续降低loss，缩放指数近乎不变；5) 幂律行为存在尺度阈值——小数据集无法反映高算力下的缩放趋势。

Conclusion: 大规模、高质量全模拟数据集是HEP缩放律研究与基础模型构建的先决条件；所提方法为HEP领域提供了可推广的缩放分析范式，揭示了多任务学习与输入表征对缩放效率的关键影响。

Abstract: Much of the recent progress in machine learning domains such as language models has come from scaling laws that predict performance as a function of training effort. In high-energy physics (HEP) similar behavior has now been observed. To aid further study, we present a systematic procedure to derive robust scaling laws and compare design choices on the relevant budget axes for HEP tasks. We first validate the full scaling trajectory on toy problems and then apply the procedure to multi-task transformers on the ~11 billion-jet ATLAS JetSet2 dataset, in both the compute- and data-constrained regimes. For the latter, we predict, to the best of our knowledge for the first time, the jointly optimal model size, training horizon, learning rate and batch size under early stopping. At compute-optimal scaling, we recover a near-equal $\sqrt{C}$ dependence of model and dataset size, and find that auxiliary objectives lower the primary jet-classification loss at equal compute budget. Expanding the inputs toward lower-level data systematically lowers the loss while leaving the scaling exponent nearly unchanged. The onset of the power-law regime is itself set by scale: below a threshold in dataset size the loss carries little information about high-compute scaling, underscoring the value of large, high-quality full-simulation datasets as a foundation for scaling studies and the development of foundation models in HEP.

</details>


### [130] [Reconfigurable On-Chip AI for Particle Physics Detectors with Logic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06816)
*Mila Bileska,Lino Gerlach,Jennet Dickinson,Lindsey Gray,Giuseppe Di Guglielmo,Peter Elmer,Isobel Ojalvo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨了逻辑神经网络（LNNs）在高能物理下一代像素探测器中的片上推理应用，强调其在资源受限、高数据率与辐射损伤环境下的高效性、鲁棒性及可重构性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代粒子物理像素探测器面临极高数据率、严苛功耗/面积约束及辐射损伤导致响应漂移的挑战，亟需兼具高能效、抗辐照性与在线可重配置能力的片上智能推理方案。

Method: 评估LNNs在像素探测器片上推理中的适用性；分析其硬件资源效率与抗单粒子翻转（SEU）能力；提出并实现可重构LNN架构以支持部署后模型更新。

Result: LNNs显著提升单位硬件资源下的模型复杂度上限；展现出对辐射诱导随机比特翻转的天然鲁棒性；所提出的可重构LNN支持运行时逻辑单元重映射与权重动态加载。

Conclusion: LNNs为面向高能物理前端探测器的自适应、抗辐照、超低功耗边缘智能提供了可行技术路径，是传统浮点/定点DNN在极端约束场景下的有力替代方案。

Abstract: On-device machine learning is increasingly important in applications where extreme data rates or resource constraints make centralized processing infeasible. Logic neural networks (LNNs), which directly learn Boolean logic instead of conventional arithmetic operations, have recently emerged as a state-of-the-art approach for efficient inference and are particularly well suited for deployment in custom silicon. However, hard-wired solutions are typically optimized for narrowly defined tasks and lack the ability to adapt to changing data distributions. Next-generation pixel detectors in particle physics present a particularly challenging use case: their high granularity and readout frequency generate data rates that cannot be centrally processed, while stringent space and power constraints necessitate highly efficient on-chip inference. Moreover, radiation damage progressively alters detector response, requiring models that can be re-trained and reconfigured after deployment. In this work, we evaluate LNNs for on-chip inference in pixel detectors. We show that their superior resource efficiency may enable a step change in model capability, making more complex architectures feasible within the available hardware budget. LNNs also provide increased robustness against radiation-induced random bit flips. Finally, we present novel work on reconfigurable LNNs, enabling models to adapt to changing operating conditions after deployment.

</details>

### [131] [Learning Low-Order Approximations of the Quark Propagator in Lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06612)
*Simon Pfahler,Maren Käfferlein,Daniel Knüttel,Christoph Lehner,Tilo Wettig*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于规范协变神经网络（Clifford基表示）的人工科学发现框架，用于提取可解释的夸克传播子近似；该方法在计算代价相同的情况下优于传统跳变展开，并在后者发散的轻夸克区域展现出收敛性，从而揭示了一种新的级数展开形式，且具有跨规范场与体积的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跳变展开作为格点QCD中夸克传播子的重要近似，在轻夸克情形下发散，严重限制了强耦合与有限密度模拟的应用范围。

Method: 采用基于Clifford代数表示的规范协变神经网络构建人工科学发现框架，以学习并提取物理可解释、规范协变的低阶传播子近似表达式。

Result: 获得比跳变展开更高精度的低阶近似；在跳变展开失效的参数区域（如小质量、强耦合）仍保持收敛；所得近似具备跨不同规范组态与格点体积的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动但物理约束（规范协变性、Clifford结构）引导的框架，不仅提供了实用的插件式传播子近似器，还启发了一种新的、收敛的夸克传播子级数展开理论范式。

Abstract: The hopping expansion is an important approximation of the quark propagator in lattice quantum chromodynamics, but it diverges for light quarks, which limits the scope of strong-coupling and finite-density simulations. We present an artificial scientific discovery framework using gauge-equivariant neural networks in the Clifford basis to extract interpretable approximations. Our approach yields improved low-order approximations that outperform the hopping expansion at identical computational cost. In the parameter regime where the hopping expansion fails, our approach identifies convergent behavior, thus suggesting a novel series expansion. The discovered approximations generalize to unseen gauge fields and volumes, enabling immediate implementation as plug-in replacements and a new theoretical understanding of quark-propagator expansions.

</details>
